These tests pin the Slice-0 invariants:\n6\t  - a legacy store is byte-identical (no manifest / segments dir created; data stays in spine.jsonl);\n7\t  - the in-process RLock is keyed on the STABLE store.path and survives a migration (invariant 14 — the\n8\t    lock `envelope.consume` shares with `append` is the same object across a rotation);\n9\t  - migrate() renames spine.jsonl → segments/seg-00000000.jsonl (O(1)) + publishes a manifest, preserving\n10\t    the chain, next_seq, and verify();\n11\t  - a STALE appender constructed before a concurrent migrate() re-resolves the active segment under the\n12\t    lock and never resurrects spine.jsonl (the split-brain the two-step migration guards against).\n13\tRun: ~/.sigil/venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_spine_rotation.py -q\n14\t\"\"\"\n15\timport gzip\n16\timport json\n17\timport os\n18\timport shutil\n19\timport tempfile\n20\timport threading\n21\timport time\n22\tfrom pathlib import Path\n23\t\n24\tfrom sigil.reuse.chain import _GENESIS_PREV\n25\tfrom sigil.spine.manifest import Manifest, Segment, read_manifest, write_manifest, SpineLayout\n26\tfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineError, SpineStore, spine_lock\n27\t\n28\t\n29\tdef _fresh_dir(prefix: str) -> Path:\n30\t    return Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=prefix))\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef _append_n(store: SpineStore, n: int, start: int = 0) -> None:\n34\t    for i in range(start, start + n):\n35\t        store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"u\", payload={\"n\": i})\n36\t\n37\t\n38\tdef _make_segmented(d: Path, seg_counts: list[int]) -> SpineLayout:\n39\t    \"\"\"Build a real chain of sum(seg_counts) records, then physically lay it out as len(seg_counts)\n40\t    segments (the last is the ACTIVE) with a valid manifest — a genuine multi-segment spine of exactly the\n41\t    shape Slice 3's rotation will produce. The chain spans every seam (each segment's first record's\n42\t    prev_hash == the prior segment's last entry_hash), because the records were ONE chain before the split.\"\"\"\n43\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n44\t    s = SpineStore(p)\n45\t    _append_n(s, sum(seg_counts))\n46\t    lay = SpineLayout.for_path(p)\n47\t    lines = [ln for ln in p.read_bytes().splitlines(keepends=True) if ln.strip()]\n48\t    recs = [json.loads(ln) for ln in lines]\n49\t    assert len(recs) == sum(seg_counts)\n50\t    lay.segments_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n51\t    segments, idx, prev_boundary, prev_seq = [], 0, _GENESIS_PREV, -1\n52\t    for seg_id, cnt in enumerate(seg_counts):\n53\t        seg_lines, seg_recs = lines[idx:idx + cnt], recs[idx:idx + cnt]\n54\t        (lay.segments_dir / f\"seg-{seg_id:08d}.jsonl\").write_bytes(b\"\".join(seg_lines))\n55\t        is_last = seg_id == len(seg_counts) - 1\n56\t        first_seq = seg_recs[0][\"seq\"] if seg_recs else prev_seq + 1   # empty active -> next seq after boundary\n57\t        segments.append(Segment(\n58\t            id=seg_id, file=f\"{lay.segments_dir.name}/seg-{seg_id:08d}.jsonl\", codec=\"none\",\n59\t            sealed=not is_last, first_seq=first_seq,\n60\t            last_seq=None if is_last else seg_recs[-1][\"seq\"],\n61\t            count=None if is_last else cnt,\n62\t            first_prev_hash=prev_boundary,\n63\t            boundary_hash=None if is_last else seg_recs[-1][\"entry_hash\"],\n64\t        ))\n65\t        if seg_recs:\n66\t            prev_boundary, prev_seq = seg_recs[-1][\"entry_hash\"], seg_recs[-1][\"seq\"]\n67\t        idx += cnt\n68\t    p.unlink()                                             # the legacy file is now split into segments\n69\t    write_manifest(lay, Manifest(generation=1, scope=\"\", segments=segments))\n70\t    return lay\n71\t\n72\t\n73\tdef test_reads_span_multiple_segments():\n74\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-span-\")\n75\t    _make_segmented(d, [3, 4, 2])                          # seg-0(seq0-2) seg-1(seq3-6) sealed; seg-2(seq7-8) active\n76\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n77\t    s = SpineStore(p)\n78\t\n79\t    ok, reason = s.verify()\n80\t    assert ok, reason                                      # verify_chain spans all 3 segments from genesis\n81\t    assert s.count() == 9\n82\t    assert [r.seq for r in s.iter_records()] == list(range(9))\n83\t    for seq in range(9):                                   # get() resolves a seq in EVERY segment\n84\t        assert s.get(seq) is not None and s.get(seq).payload[\"n\"] == seq\n85\t    assert [r.seq for r in s.iter_records(since_seq=2)] == [3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8]   # seeks past seg-0\n86\t    assert [r.seq for r in s.iter_records(since_seq=6)] == [7, 8]               # into the active\n87\t    assert [r.seq for r in s.tail(5)] == [4, 5, 6, 7, 8]   # tail() spans the seg-1→seg-2 seam (B3)\n88\t    assert s.next_seq == 9                                 # tip from the active segment\n89\t\n90\t    s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"u\", payload={\"n\": 9})   # append continues across the last seam\n91\t    fresh = SpineStore(p)\n92\t    ok, reason = fresh.verify()\n93\t    assert ok, reason\n94\t    assert [r.seq for r in fresh.iter_records()] == list(range(10))\n95\t\n96\t\n97\tdef test_missing_referenced_segment_raises():\n98\t    import pytest\n99\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-missing-\")\n100\t    lay = _make_segmented(d, [3, 2])\n101\t    (lay.segments_dir / \"seg-00000000.jsonl\").unlink()     # a sealed segment vanishes → fail closed\n102\t    s = SpineStore(d / \"spine.jsonl\")\n103\t    with pytest.raises(SpineError):\n104\t        list(s.iter_records())\n105\t    with pytest.raises(SpineError):\n106\t        s.verify()\n107\t\n108\t\n109\tdef test_empty_active_seam_no_seq0_fork():\n110\t    \"\"\"A rotated (non-genesis) active segment that is EMPTY must seed its first append from the prior\n111\t    sealed segment's boundary — NOT build_chain from genesis (which would fork the chain at seq 0).\"\"\"\n112\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-seam-\")\n113\t    lay = _make_segmented(d, [4, 0])                       # seg-0 sealed (seq0-3); seg-1 active but EMPTY\n114\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n115\t    s = SpineStore(p)\n116\t    assert s.next_seq == 4, \"tip resolves across the empty active to the sealed boundary\"\n117\t    s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"u\", payload={\"n\": 4})   # must become seq 4, prev=seg-0 boundary\n118\t    ok, reason = SpineStore(p).verify()\n119\t    assert ok, reason\n120\t    assert [r.seq for r in SpineStore(p).iter_records()] == [0, 1, 2, 3, 4], \"no seq-0 fork across the seam\"\n121\t\n122\t\n123\tdef test_legacy_store_is_byte_identical():\n124\t    \"\"\"No manifest ⇒ active target IS the legacy single file; no manifest/segments artifacts appear.\"\"\"\n125\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-legacy-\")\n126\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n127\t    s = SpineStore(p)\n128\t    _append_n(s, 4)\n129\t    lay = SpineLayout.for_path(p)\n130\t    assert s._active == p, \"legacy store must append/read in place at spine.jsonl\"\n131\t    assert p.exists()\n132\t    assert not lay.manifest_path.exists(), \"no manifest is written until an explicit migrate()\"\n133\t    assert not lay.segments_dir.exists(), \"segments/ is not created for a legacy/fresh store\"\n134\t    ok, reason = s.verify()\n135\t    assert ok, reason\n136\t    assert [r.seq for r in s.iter_records()] == [0, 1, 2, 3]\n137\t\n138\t\n139\tdef test_rlock_keyed_on_stable_path_and_survives_migration():\n140\t    \"\"\"Invariant 14: the RLock is keyed on the stable store.path, so the object `append` and\n141\t    `envelope.consume` both take via spine_lock(store.path) is identical — and stays identical across a\n142\t    migration (store.path never changes, even though spine.jsonl is renamed away).\"\"\"\n143\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-rlock-\")\n144\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n145\t    s = SpineStore(p)\n146\t    _append_n(s, 2)\n147\t    lock_before = spine_lock(s.path)\n148\t    assert spine_lock(s.path) is lock_before, \"same path ⇒ same RLock object\"\n149\t    assert s.migrate() is True\n150\t    assert s.path == p, \"store.path (the lock/identity token) is unchanged by migration\"\n151\t    assert spine_lock(s.path) is lock_before, \"the RLock survives the migration unchanged\"\n152\t\n153\t\n154\tdef test_migrate_preserves_chain_and_moves_to_segment():\n155\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-mig-\")\n156\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n157\t    s = SpineStore(p)\n158\t    _append_n(s, 5)\n159\t    before = [(r.seq, r.entry_hash, r.payload[\"n\"]) for r in s.iter_records()]\n160\t\n161\t    lay = SpineLayout.for_path(p)\n162\t    assert s.migrate() is True\n163\t    assert not p.exists(), \"spine.jsonl is renamed away and never a data file again\"\n164\t    assert (lay.segments_dir / \"seg-00000000.jsonl\").exists()\n165\t    assert lay.manifest_path.exists()\n166\t\n167\t    # reads identical, chain intact, tip preserved\n168\t    assert [(r.seq, r.entry_hash, r.payload[\"n\"]) for r in s.iter_records()] == before\n169\t    ok, reason = s.verify()\n170\t    assert ok, reason\n171\t    assert s.next_seq == 5\n172\t\n173\t    # a FRESH instance resolves the active segment via the manifest and reads the same chain\n174\t    fresh = SpineStore(p)\n175\t    assert [r.seq for r in fresh.iter_records()] == [0, 1, 2, 3, 4]\n176\t    assert fresh.get(2) is not None and fresh.get(2).payload[\"n\"] == 2\n177\t\n178\t    # appends continue cleanly with no seq reuse\n179\t    s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"u\", payload={\"n\": 5})\n180\t    ok, reason = SpineStore(p).verify()\n181\t    assert ok, reason\n182\t    assert [r.seq for r in SpineStore(p).iter_records()] == [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5]\n183\t\n184\t\n185\tdef test_migrate_is_idempotent():\n186\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-idem-\")\n187\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n188\t    s = SpineStore(p)\n189\t    _append_n(s, 3)\n190\t    assert s.migrate() is True\n191\t    gen1 = read_manifest(SpineLayout.for_path(p)).generation\n192\t    assert s.migrate() is False, \"a second migrate is a no-op\"\n193\t    assert read_manifest(SpineLayout.for_path(p)).generation == gen1\n194\t\n195\t\n196\tdef test_migrate_fresh_creates_empty_active():\n197\t    \"\"\"A fresh (never-appended) store migrates by creating an empty active segment; appends then work.\"\"\"\n198\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-freshmig-\")\n199\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n200\t    s = SpineStore(p)\n201\t    assert s.migrate() is True\n202\t    assert not p.exists()\n203\t    seg0 = SpineLayout.for_path(p).segments_dir / \"seg-00000000.jsonl\"\n204\t    assert seg0.exists() and seg0.stat().st_size == 0\n205\t    assert s.next_seq == 0\n206\t    _append_n(s, 2)\n207\t    ok, reason = SpineStore(p).verify()\n208\t    assert ok, reason\n209\t    assert [r.seq for r in SpineStore(p).iter_records()] == [0, 1]\n210\t\n211\t\n212\tdef test_orphan_migration_is_reconciled():\n213\t    \"\"\"The judge-flagged migration crash window: a crash AFTER the atomic rename but BEFORE the manifest\n214\t    write leaves seg-0 present, spine.jsonl gone, no manifest. The store MUST reconcile (finish the\n215\t    migration) and recover the records — never read the spine as empty and hide all history.\"\"\"\n216\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-orphan-\")\n217\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n218\t    s = SpineStore(p)\n219\t    _append_n(s, 4)\n220\t    before = [r.seq for r in s.iter_records()]\n221\t    lay = SpineLayout.for_path(p)\n222\t\n223\t    # simulate the crash: rename done, manifest NOT written\n224\t    lay.segments_dir.mkdir()\n225\t    os.replace(p, lay.segments_dir / \"seg-00000000.jsonl\")\n226\t    assert not p.exists() and (lay.segments_dir / \"seg-00000000.jsonl\").exists()\n227\t    assert not lay.manifest_path.exists()\n228\t\n229\t    s2 = SpineStore(p)                                     # __init__ must reconcile\n230\t    assert lay.manifest_path.exists(), \"reconciler publishes the manifest to complete the migration\"\n231\t    assert [r.seq for r in s2.iter_records()] == before, \"records recovered, NOT hidden as an empty spine\"\n232\t    ok, reason = s2.verify()\n233\t    assert ok, reason\n234\t\n235\t\n236\tdef test_live_appender_completes_orphan_migration_no_resurrect():\n237\t    \"\"\"The CRITICAL review finding: a long-lived appender (e.g. the bridge) that never re-constructed its\n238\t    store, appending AFTER another process crashed mid-migrate(), must COMPLETE the orphan under the lock\n239\t    and write to the migrated segment — never re-create spine.jsonl and fork the log.\"\"\"\n240\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-orphanlive-\")\n241\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n242\t    lay = SpineLayout.for_path(p)\n243\t    a = SpineStore(p)                                      # long-lived appender, legacy _active == spine.jsonl\n244\t    _append_n(a, 3)\n245\t    assert a._active == p\n246\t\n247\t    # simulate a crash mid-migrate() in ANOTHER process: rename done, manifest NOT written\n248\t    lay.segments_dir.mkdir()\n249\t    os.replace(p, lay.segments_dir / \"seg-00000000.jsonl\")\n250\t    assert not p.exists()\n251\t\n252\t    a.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"u\", payload={\"n\": 3})   # A never saw the migration\n253\t    assert not p.exists(), \"the live appender must NOT resurrect spine.jsonl (fork)\"\n254\t    assert lay.manifest_path.exists(), \"the live appender completed the interrupted migration under the lock\"\n255\t    recs = [r.payload[\"n\"] for r in SpineStore(p).iter_records()]\n256\t    assert recs == [0, 1, 2, 3], f\"no fork — all records in the migrated segment: {recs}\"\n257\t    ok, reason = SpineStore(p).verify()\n258\t    assert ok, reason\n259\t\n260\t\n261\tdef test_reader_sees_data_during_pre_manifest_migrate_window():\n262\t    \"\"\"F3 completeness (re-check finding): a lock-free reader with a stale active (constructed before the\n263\t    migration) must NOT see a false-empty spine during the migrate() window where spine.jsonl is already\n264\t    renamed to seg-0 but the manifest is not yet published — else the kill-switch scan and nonce highwater\n265\t    regress (the un-halting / replay direction).\"\"\"\n266\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-premanifest-\")\n267\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n268\t    lay = SpineLayout.for_path(p)\n269\t    a = SpineStore(p)                                      # stale reader; _active == spine.jsonl\n270\t    _append_n(a, 4)\n271\t\n272\t    # reproduce the IN-migrate window: rename done, manifest NOT yet written\n273\t    lay.segments_dir.mkdir()\n274\t    os.replace(p, lay.segments_dir / \"seg-00000000.jsonl\")\n275\t    assert not p.exists() and not lay.manifest_path.exists()\n276\t\n277\t    assert [r.seq for r in a.iter_records()] == [0, 1, 2, 3], \"reader must not see a false-empty spine mid-migrate\"\n278\t    assert a.count() == 4\n279\t    assert a.get(2) is not None and a.get(2).payload[\"n\"] == 2          # indexed read path must not crash\n280\t\n281\t\n282\tdef test_change_token_reflects_append_and_migration():\n283\t    \"\"\"change_token() (the unified A4 epoch) must move on every append, on migration, and on a post-\n284\t    migration append — so a bare file-size check that would freeze once spine.jsonl is renamed away is\n285\t    replaced by a token that keeps changing.\"\"\"\n286\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-tok-\")\n287\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n288\t    s = SpineStore(p)\n289\t    t0 = s.change_token()\n290\t    s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"u\", payload={\"n\": 0})\n291\t    t1 = s.change_token()\n292\t    assert t1 != t0, \"append changes the token\"\n293\t    s.migrate()\n294\t    t2 = s.change_token()\n295\t    assert t2 != t1, \"migration changes the token (generation + active path)\"\n296\t    s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"u\", payload={\"n\": 1})\n297\t    assert s.change_token() != t2, \"append after migration changes the token\"\n298\t\n299\t\n300\tdef test_gesture_killswitch_panic_observed_after_migration():\n301\t    \"\"\"BLOCK-2 end-to-end: a kill-switch PANIC engaged AFTER a migration must be observed by a running\n302\t    gesture session. The old size-token froze on OSError once spine.jsonl was renamed away — stranding a\n303\t    device-armed session un-halted. The change token keeps the panic observable.\"\"\"\n304\t    from sigil.gesture.session import SessionGate\n305\t\n306\t    class _NullBackend:\n307\t        def move(self, *a, **k): pass\n308\t        def click(self, *a, **k): pass\n309\t        def type_text(self, *a, **k): pass\n310\t        def launch(self, *a, **k): pass\n311\t\n312\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-kssess-\")\n313\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n314\t    s = SpineStore(p)\n315\t    g = SessionGate(s, _NullBackend())\n316\t    assert g._killswitch_engaged(now=1.0) is False\n317\t    s.migrate()                                            # spine.jsonl renamed away\n318\t    from sigil.governor.killswitch import KillSwitch\n319\t    KillSwitch(s).engage(by=\"owner\", reason=\"post-migration panic\")\n320\t    assert g._killswitch_engaged(now=100.0) is True, \\\n321\t        \"a panic after migration must be observed (a frozen size token would strand the session un-halted)\"\n322\t\n323\t\n324\tdef test_manifest_rejects_path_escaping_segment_file():\n325\t    \"\"\"MEDIUM-1: a doctored manifest whose segment `file` escapes the spine dir (absolute or `..`) is\n326\t    rejected at the model boundary, because get()/iter/tail open segment files without running verify().\"\"\"\n327\t    import pytest\n328\t    from pydantic import ValidationError\n329\t\n330\t    from sigil.spine.manifest import Segment\n331\t\n332\t    for bad in (\"/etc/hostname\", \"../../etc/hostname\", \"../secret.jsonl\"):\n333\t        with pytest.raises(ValidationError):\n334\t            Segment(id=0, file=bad, first_seq=0)\n335\t    Segment(id=0, file=\"spine.segments/seg-00000000.jsonl\", first_seq=0)   # a contained relative path is fine\n336\t\n337\t\n338\tdef test_reset_clears_all_segment_artifacts():\n339\t    \"\"\"reset() must clear the legacy file, manifest, ALL segments, trash, and lockfile — not just\n340\t    spine.jsonl (the old --reset left rotated segments + the manifest behind, resurrecting stale data).\"\"\"\n341\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-reset-\")\n342\t    lay = _make_segmented(d, [3, 2])                       # a migrated, multi-segment spine + manifest\n343\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n344\t    # touch a lockfile + trash to prove they are cleared too\n345\t    _append_n(SpineStore(p), 0)                            # (constructs; no-op append count)\n346\t    lay.trash_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n347\t    (lay.trash_dir / \"old.jsonl\").write_text(\"x\")\n348\t    assert lay.manifest_path.exists() and lay.segments_dir.exists()\n349\t\n350\t    SpineStore(p).reset()\n351\t    assert not lay.manifest_path.exists(), \"manifest cleared\"\n352\t    assert not lay.segments_dir.exists(), \"segments dir cleared\"\n353\t    assert not lay.trash_dir.exists(), \"trash cleared\"\n354\t    assert not p.exists(), \"legacy file cleared\"\n355\t    # a fresh store reads empty and can be rebuilt\n356\t    s = SpineStore(p)\n357\t    assert s.count() == 0 and s.next_seq == 0\n358\t    _append_n(s, 2)\n359\t    ok, reason = SpineStore(p).verify()\n360\t    assert ok, reason\n361\t\n362\t\n363\tdef test_cli_spine_migrate_and_status(capsys):\n364\t    \"\"\"The `sigil spine migrate|status` wrappers operate on the DEFAULT store (isolated under the test\n365\t    SIGIL_HOME). Bracketed with reset() so it neither inherits nor leaks default-spine state.\"\"\"\n366\t    from sigil.cli import cmd_spine\n367\t\n368\t    def _ns(**kw):\n369\t        return type(\"A\", (), kw)()\n370\t\n371\t    SpineStore().reset()                                   # clean legacy start on the default path\n372\t    try:\n373\t        _append_n(SpineStore(), 3)\n374\t        cmd_spine(_ns(action=\"status\"))\n375\t        assert \"LEGACY single file\" in capsys.readouterr().out\n376\t        cmd_spine(_ns(action=\"migrate\"))\n377\t        assert \"migrated\" in capsys.readouterr().out\n378\t        cmd_spine(_ns(action=\"migrate\"))                   # idempotent\n379\t        assert \"already migrated\" in capsys.readouterr().out\n380\t        cmd_spine(_ns(action=\"status\"))\n381\t        out = capsys.readouterr().out\n382\t        assert \"1 segment(s)\" in out and \"generation 0\" in out and \"3 records\" in out\n383\t    finally:\n384\t        SpineStore().reset()                               # don't leak a migrated default spine to other tests\n385\t\n386\t\n387\tdef test_rotation_seals_into_contiguous_segments():\n388\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-rot-\")\n389\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n390\t    s = SpineStore(p, seg_max_bytes=0, seg_max_records=4)   # seal every 4 records\n391\t    s.migrate()\n392\t    _append_n(s, 18)\n393\t    info = s.segment_info()\n394\t    assert len(info) >= 4, f\"expected several sealed segments, got {len(info)}\"\n395\t    assert sum(1 for x in info if not x[\"sealed\"]) == 1, \"exactly one active segment\"\n396\t    ok, reason = SpineStore(p).verify()\n397\t    assert ok, reason                                       # chain spans every seam from genesis\n398\t    assert [r.seq for r in SpineStore(p).iter_records()] == list(range(18))\n399\t    assert SpineStore(p).count() == 18 and SpineStore(p).next_seq == 18\n400\t    for seq in (0, 5, 11, 17):                              # get() across sealed + active segments\n401\t        assert SpineStore(p).get(seq).payload[\"n\"] == seq\n402\t\n403\t\n404\tdef test_legacy_store_never_auto_rotates():\n405\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-norot-\")\n406\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n407\t    s = SpineStore(p, seg_max_bytes=0, seg_max_records=2)   # tiny threshold, but NOT migrated\n408\t    _append_n(s, 20)\n409\t    assert s.segment_info() == [], \"a legacy (un-migrated) spine never rotates\"\n410\t    assert s._active == p and p.exists()\n411\t    ok, reason = s.verify()\n412\t    assert ok, reason\n413\t\n414\t\n415\tdef test_rotation_disabled_when_thresholds_zero():\n416\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-rotoff-\")\n417\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n418\t    s = SpineStore(p, seg_max_bytes=0, seg_max_records=0)   # both bounds off => never rotate\n419\t    s.migrate()\n420\t    _append_n(s, 30)\n421\t    assert len(s.segment_info()) == 1, \"rotation disabled: one (growing) active segment\"\n422\t    ok, reason = SpineStore(p).verify()\n423\t    assert ok, reason\n424\t\n425\t\n426\tdef test_manual_rotate():\n427\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-manrot-\")\n428\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n429\t    s = SpineStore(p, seg_max_bytes=0, seg_max_records=0)   # no auto-rotation\n430\t    s.migrate()\n431\t    _append_n(s, 5)\n432\t    assert s.rotate() is True                               # force a seal\n433\t    assert len(s.segment_info()) == 2\n434\t    _append_n(s, 3, start=5)\n435\t    ok, reason = SpineStore(p).verify()\n436\t    assert ok, reason\n437\t    assert [r.seq for r in SpineStore(p).iter_records()] == list(range(8))\n438\t\n439\t\n440\tdef test_ambient_crash_env_is_inert_in_production(monkeypatch):\n441\t    \"\"\"Review fix: the fault-injection hook must NOT be armable by an ambient env var — production ships\n442\t    _crash_hook=None, so setting SIGIL_SPINE_CRASH_AT does nothing (no os._exit crash-loop of every writer).\"\"\"\n443\t    import sigil.spine.store as store\n444\t    assert store._crash_hook is None, \"production must ship no crash hook\"\n445\t    monkeypatch.setenv(\"SIGIL_SPINE_CRASH_AT\", \"append_after_fsync\")\n446\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-nohook-\")\n447\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n448\t    s = SpineStore(p, seg_max_records=2)\n449\t    s.migrate()\n450\t    assert s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"u\", payload={\"n\": 0}) == 0   # must NOT os._exit\n451\t\n452\t\n453\tdef test_negative_threshold_clamps_to_disabled():\n454\t    \"\"\"Review fix: a negative threshold clamps to 0 (disabled), not `size >= -1` == always-over (which\n455\t    would seal on every append — a self-DoS).\"\"\"\n456\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-negthr-\")\n457\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n458\t    s = SpineStore(p, seg_max_bytes=-1, seg_max_records=-1)\n459\t    assert s._seg_max_bytes == 0 and s._seg_max_records == 0\n460\t    s.migrate()\n461\t    _append_n(s, 10)\n462\t    assert len(s.segment_info()) == 1, \"clamped to disabled — one segment, not one-per-record\"\n463\t\n464\t\n465\tdef test_panic_append_durable_when_it_triggers_rotation():\n466\t    \"\"\"D1 / write-then-rotate: a kill-switch PANIC append that itself trips the rotation threshold is\n467\t    written + fsync'd BEFORE the seal, so it is durable and honored even though it triggered a rotation.\"\"\"\n468\t    from sigil.governor.killswitch import KillSwitch\n469\t\n470\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-panicrot-\")\n471\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n472\t    s = SpineStore(p, seg_max_bytes=0, seg_max_records=3)\n473\t    s.migrate()\n474\t    _append_n(s, 2)                                         # records 0,1 in the active\n475\t    KillSwitch(s).engage(by=\"owner\", reason=\"panic\")        # record 2 -> trips the seal (write-then-rotate)\n476\t    assert KillSwitch(SpineStore(p)).is_engaged() is True, \"panic honored across the rotation it triggered\"\n477\t    assert len(SpineStore(p).segment_info()) >= 2, \"the seal happened\"\n478\t    ok, reason = SpineStore(p).verify()\n479\t    assert ok, reason\n480\t\n481\t\n482\tdef test_compact_gzips_sealed_and_reads_transparently():\n483\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-compact-\")\n484\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n485\t    lay = SpineLayout.for_path(p)\n486\t    s = SpineStore(p, seg_max_bytes=0, seg_max_records=40)\n487\t    s.migrate()\n488\t    for i in range(180):\n489\t        s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"a\", payload={\"n\": i, \"text\": \"lorem ipsum dolor sit amet \" * 3})\n490\t    before = sum(f.stat().st_size for f in lay.segments_dir.glob(\"*.jsonl\"))\n491\t    n = s.compact()\n492\t    assert n >= 3, f\"expected several sealed segments compressed, got {n}\"\n493\t    after = sum(f.stat().st_size for f in lay.segments_dir.glob(\"*.gz\"))\n494\t    assert after < before, f\"gzip must reclaim disk: {after} !< {before}\"\n495\t    codecs = {x[\"id\"]: x[\"codec\"] for x in s.segment_info()}\n496\t    assert all(codecs[i] == \"gzip\" for i in codecs if i != max(codecs)), \"all sealed segments gzip\"\n497\t    assert codecs[max(codecs)] == \"none\", \"active segment stays plaintext\"\n498\t\n499\t    # every read is transparent across gz + plaintext\n500\t    fresh = SpineStore(p)\n501\t    ok, reason = fresh.verify()\n502\t    assert ok, reason\n503\t    assert [r.seq for r in fresh.iter_records()] == list(range(180))\n504\t    assert fresh.count() == 180 and fresh.next_seq == 180\n505\t    for seq in (0, 41, 179):                                # get() from a gz segment and the active\n506\t        assert fresh.get(seq).payload[\"n\"] == seq\n507\t    assert [r.seq for r in fresh.tail(3)] == [177, 178, 179]\n508\t    assert s.compact() == 0, \"idempotent — nothing left to compact\"\n509\t    # a post-compact append still verifies and rotation still works over gz segments\n510\t    s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"a\", payload={\"n\": 180})\n511\t    ok, reason = SpineStore(p).verify()\n512\t    assert ok, reason\n513\t\n514\t\n515\tdef test_reader_grace_during_concurrent_compact():\n516\t    \"\"\"D2: lock-free readers iterating while a compactor gzips sealed segments (moving superseded plaintext\n517\t    to trash) must NEVER ENOENT or see a short/forked chain — the manifest-reread retry + open-fd grace\n518\t    keep every read a contiguous chain from genesis.\"\"\"\n519\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-d2-\")\n520\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n521\t    s = SpineStore(p, seg_max_bytes=0, seg_max_records=15)\n522\t    s.migrate()\n523\t    _append_n(s, 60)\n524\t\n525\t    errors: list[str] = []\n526\t    stop = threading.Event()\n527\t\n528\t    def reader():\n529\t        r = SpineStore(p)\n530\t        while not stop.is_set():\n531\t            try:\n532\t                seqs = [rec.seq for rec in r.iter_records()]\n533\t            except Exception as e:                          # noqa: BLE001 — any read exception is a failure\n534\t                errors.append(f\"reader raised {e!r}\")\n535\t                return\n536\t            if seqs != list(range(len(seqs))):\n537\t                errors.append(f\"reader saw a non-contiguous chain: {seqs[:3]}..{seqs[-3:]}\")\n538\t                return\n539\t\n540\t    threads = [threading.Thread(target=reader) for _ in range(4)]\n541\t    for t in threads:\n542\t        t.start()\n543\t    for k in range(4):                                      # interleave: more sealed plaintext, then compact\n544\t        _append_n(s, 15, start=60 + k * 15)\n545\t        s.compact()\n546\t        time.sleep(0.005)\n547\t    stop.set()\n548\t    for t in threads:\n549\t        t.join(timeout=10)\n550\t    assert not errors, errors[:3]\n551\t\n552\t\n553\tdef test_reap_trash_respects_grace(monkeypatch):\n554\t    import sigil.spine.store as store_mod\n555\t\n556\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-reap-\")\n557\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n558\t    lay = SpineLayout.for_path(p)\n559\t    s = SpineStore(p, seg_max_bytes=0, seg_max_records=5)\n560\t    s.migrate()\n561\t    _append_n(s, 12)\n562\t    s.compact()\n563\t    assert list(lay.trash_dir.iterdir()), \"compact trashes superseded plaintext\"\n564\t    s._reap_trash()                                         # default grace (1h) => young trash survives\n565\t    assert list(lay.trash_dir.iterdir()), \"trash within the read-grace is NOT reaped\"\n566\t    monkeypatch.setattr(store_mod, \"SPINE_TRASH_GRACE_SEC\", -1)\n567\t    s._reap_trash()                                         # grace -1 => everything is past grace\n568\t    assert not list(lay.trash_dir.iterdir()), \"trash past the grace is reaped\"\n569\t\n570\t\n571\tdef test_compact_recovers_from_crash_before_codec_flip():\n572\t    \"\"\"Crash after building the .gz but before the manifest flip: an orphan .gz, manifest still plaintext.\n573\t    Reads use the plaintext; re-running compact overwrites the orphan and completes.\"\"\"\n574\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-ccrash1-\")\n575\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n576\t    lay = SpineLayout.for_path(p)\n577\t    s = SpineStore(p, seg_max_bytes=0, seg_max_records=5)\n578\t    s.migrate()\n579\t    _append_n(s, 12)\n580\t    plain0 = lay.segments_dir / \"seg-00000000.jsonl\"\n581\t    with plain0.open(\"rb\") as fin, gzip.open(lay.segments_dir / \"seg-00000000.jsonl.gz\", \"wb\") as fout:\n582\t        shutil.copyfileobj(fin, fout)                       # simulate the orphan .gz (crashed before flip)\n583\t    ok, reason = SpineStore(p).verify()\n584\t    assert ok, reason                                       # manifest still plaintext -> reads use it\n585\t    s.compact()\n586\t    assert s.segment_info()[0][\"codec\"] == \"gzip\"\n587\t    ok, reason = SpineStore(p).verify()\n588\t    assert ok, reason\n589\t\n590\t\n591\tdef test_compact_sweeps_orphan_plaintext_after_flip():\n592\t    \"\"\"Crash after the codec flip but before the plaintext move: an orphan plaintext beside the gz. The\n593\t    next compact()'s orphan-sweep trashes it.\"\"\"\n594\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-ccrash2-\")\n595\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n596\t    lay = SpineLayout.for_path(p)\n597\t    s = SpineStore(p, seg_max_bytes=0, seg_max_records=5)\n598\t    s.migrate()\n599\t    _append_n(s, 12)\n600\t    s.compact()                                            # seg-0 -> gzip, plaintext trashed\n601\t    (lay.segments_dir / \"seg-00000000.jsonl\").write_bytes(b'{\"stale\":true}\\n')   # crash-stranded plaintext\n602\t    s.compact()                                            # orphan-sweep must trash it\n603\t    assert not (lay.segments_dir / \"seg-00000000.jsonl\").exists(), \"orphan plaintext swept to trash\"\n604\t    ok, reason = SpineStore(p).verify()\n605\t    assert ok, reason\n606\t\n607\t\n608\tdef test_reset_keeps_lockfile_so_it_still_excludes(monkeypatch):\n609\t    \"\"\"Review F1: reset() must NOT unlink the lockfile it holds (flock binds to the inode, not the path),\n610\t    or a concurrent appender opens a new inode and slips the lock during the destructive rmtree. Assert the\n611\t    lockfile survives reset so its flock keeps excluding appenders for the whole critical section.\"\"\"\n612\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-resetlock-\")\n613\t    _make_segmented(d, [3, 2])\n614\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n615\t    lay = SpineLayout.for_path(p)\n616\t    SpineStore(p).append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"u\", payload={\"n\": 99})   # ensure the lockfile exists\n617\t    assert lay.lockfile_path.exists()\n618\t    SpineStore(p).reset()\n619\t    assert lay.lockfile_path.exists(), \"reset() must keep the path-stable lockfile (it holds its flock)\"\n620\t    assert not lay.manifest_path.exists() and not lay.segments_dir.exists()\n621\t\n622\t\n623\tdef test_get_fails_closed_on_missing_segment():\n624\t    \"\"\"Review F2a: get() of a seq in a SURVIVING segment must NOT fail open while the chain is truncated —\n625\t    it fails closed (raises) exactly like the scans, not returns a value from stale index state.\"\"\"\n626\t    import pytest\n627\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-getclosed-\")\n628\t    lay = _make_segmented(d, [3, 3])                       # seg-0 seq0-2 sealed; seg-1 seq3-5 active\n629\t    s = SpineStore(d / \"spine.jsonl\")\n630\t    assert s.get(4) is not None                            # warm the index while whole\n631\t    (lay.segments_dir / \"seg-00000000.jsonl\").unlink()     # a DIFFERENT segment vanishes\n632\t    with pytest.raises(SpineError):\n633\t        s.get(4)                                           # must fail closed, not return seq 4 from seg-1\n634\t\n635\t\n636\tdef test_append_fails_closed_not_fork_when_seam_unreadable():\n637\t    \"\"\"Review F3: append into an empty non-genesis active whose seam boundary is unreadable must FAIL\n638\t    CLOSED (refuse) — never build_chain from genesis and write a seq-0 fork into a first_seq>0 segment.\"\"\"\n639\t    import pytest\n640\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-seamclosed-\")\n641\t    lay = _make_segmented(d, [4, 0])                       # seg-0 sealed seq0-3; seg-1 active EMPTY first_seq=4\n642\t    (lay.segments_dir / \"seg-00000000.jsonl\").unlink()     # the seam boundary vanishes\n643\t    s = SpineStore(d / \"spine.jsonl\")\n644\t    with pytest.raises(SpineError):\n645\t        s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"u\", payload={\"n\": 4})   # must refuse, not fork at seq 0\n646\t\n647\t\n648\tdef test_stale_appender_reresolves_no_split_brain():\n649\t    \"\"\"The core split-brain test. Instance A appends in legacy mode; instance B migrates; A appends\n650\t    again. A MUST re-resolve the active segment under the lock and write to the migrated segment — never\n651\t    resurrect spine.jsonl and fork the chain.\"\"\"\n652\t    d = _fresh_dir(\"sigil-sb-\")\n653\t    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n654\t    a = SpineStore(p)\n655\t    a.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"u\", payload={\"n\": 0})\n656\t    assert a._active == p                                   # A cached the legacy path\n657\t\n658\t    b = SpineStore(p)                                       # \"another process\"\n659\t    assert b.migrate() is True                              # p is renamed → seg-0; A's cached _active is now stale\n660\t\n661\t    a.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"u\", payload={\"n\": 1})   # A must re-resolve under the lock\n662\t    assert not p.exists(), \"a stale appender must not resurrect spine.jsonl (split-brain / fork)\"\n663\t\n664\t    recs = [r.payload[\"n\"] for r in SpineStore(p).iter_records()]\n665\t    assert recs == [0, 1], f\"both records land in the migrated segment, no fork: {recs}\"\n666\t    ok, reason = SpineStore(p).verify()\n667\t    assert ok, reason\n668\t    seqs = [r.seq for r in SpineStore(p).iter_records()]\n669\t    assert seqs == [0, 1], f\"no seq reuse across the migration seam: {seqs}\"\n670\t"}]},"uuid":"30eb525d-371e-4d65-8eb2-b8c5f1cc3eb1","timestamp":"2026-07-19T18:54:23.918Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"4fa007dc-610b-42f4-b56b-4311359b0a5a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

Unlike iter_records, _read_segment_tail\nhas NO reader-grace retry — it just returns [] on `not p.exists()`.\"\"\"\nimport tempfile, threading, time\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore\n\nd = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=\"tailrace-\"))\np = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\ns = SpineStore(p, seg_max_bytes=0, seg_max_records=15)\ns.migrate()\nfor i in range(150):\n    s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"a\", payload={\"n\": i, \"pad\": \"x\" * 40})\n\nbad = []\nstop = threading.Event()\n\ndef reader():\n    r = SpineStore(p)\n    while not stop.is_set():\n        recs = r.tail(60)                      # window spans ~4 sealed segments + active\n        seqs = [rec.seq for rec in recs]\n        # tail(60) on a >=150 record spine must be exactly the last 60 seqs, contiguous\n        if seqs != list(range(seqs[0], seqs[0] + len(seqs))):\n            bad.append((\"NON-CONTIGUOUS\", seqs[:4], seqs[-4:]))\n            return\n        if len(seqs) == 60 and seqs != list(range(90, 150)):\n            bad.append((\"WRONG-WINDOW\", seqs[0], seqs[-1], len(seqs)))\n            return\n        if len(seqs) < 60:\n            bad.append((\"SHORT\", len(seqs), seqs[0] if seqs else None, seqs[-1] if seqs else None))\n            return\n\nthreads = [threading.Thread(target=reader) for _ in range(4)]\nfor t in threads:\n    t.start()\n\n# repeatedly seal more + compact so sealed plaintext keeps getting moved to trash under the readers\nfor k in range(30):\n    for i in range(15):\n        s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"a\", payload={\"n\": 150 + k * 15 + i, \"pad\": \"x\" * 40})\n    s.compact()\n    time.sleep(0.003)\n\nstop.set()\nfor t in threads:\n    t.join(timeout=10)\n\nif bad:\n    print(\"FINDING CONFIRMED — tail() returned a corrupted window under concurrent compaction:\")\n    for b in bad[:5]:\n        print(\"  \", b)\nelse:\n    print(\"no bad tail observed (race not hit this Unlike iter_records, _read_segment_tail\nhas NO reader-grace retry — it just returns [] on `not p.exists()`.\"\"\"\nimport tempfile, threading, time\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore\n\nd = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=\"tailrace-\"))\np = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\ns = SpineStore(p, seg_max_bytes=0, seg_max_records=15)\ns.migrate()\nfor i in range(150):\n    s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"a\", payload={\"n\": i, \"pad\": \"x\" * 40})\n\nbad = []\nstop = threading.Event()\n\ndef reader():\n    r = SpineStore(p)\n    while not stop.is_set():\n        recs = r.tail(60)                      # window spans ~4 sealed segments + active\n        seqs = [rec.seq for rec in recs]\n        if not seqs:\n            continue\n        if seqs != list(range(seqs[0], seqs[0] + len(seqs))):\n            bad.append((\"NON-CONTIGUOUS\", seqs[:4], seqs[-4:]))\n            return\n        if len(seqs) < 60:\n            bad.append((\"SHORT (<60 on a full spine)\", len(seqs), seqs[0], seqs[-1]))\n            return\n\nthreads = [threading.Thread(target=reader) for _ in range(4)]\nfor t in threads:\n    t.start()\n\nfor k in range(40):\n    for i in range(15):\n        s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"a\", payload={\"n\": 150 + k * 15 + i, \"pad\": \"x\" * 40})\n    s.compact()\n    time.sleep(0.003)\n\nstop.set()\nfor t in threads:\n    t.join(timeout=15)\n\nif bad:\n    print(\"FINDING CONFIRMED — tail() returned a corrupted window under concurrent compaction:\")\n    for b in bad[:5]:\n        print(\"  \", b)\nelse:\n    print(\"no bad tail observed (race not hit this A concurrent compact() that moves a sealed\nplaintext to trash in that gap makes _read_segment_tail see `not p.exists()` and SILENTLY return [] —\nthere is NO reader-grace retry (contrast iter_records). We inject the move at that exact point once.\"\"\"\nimport tempfile\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport sigil.spine.store as store_mod\nfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore\n\nd = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=\"taildet-\"))\np = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\ns = SpineStore(p, seg_max_bytes=0, seg_max_records=15)\ns.migrate()\nfor i in range(75):                                   # seg0..seg3 sealed (seq0-59), seg4 active (60-74)\n    s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"a\", payload={\"n\": i, \"pad\": \"x\" * 40})\n\n# baseline: correct tail\nr = SpineStore(p)\nbase = [rec.seq for rec in r.tail(40)]\nprint(\"baseline tail(40):\", base[0], \"..\", base[-1], \"len\", len(base),\n      \"contiguous\" if base == list(range(base[0], base[0] + len(base))) else \"NON-CONTIGUOUS\")\n\n# Inject the race: the instant tail() goes to read one sealed plaintext segment, a concurrent compactor\n# moves that plaintext to the trash dir (exactly what _trash_orphan_plaintext does after a flip).\norig = SpineStore._read_segment_tail\nfired = {\"done\": False}\ndef racy(self, seg_path, k):\n    if (not fired[\"done\"]) and seg_path.suffix != \".gz\" \\\n            and seg_path.parent == self._layout.segments_dir and \"seg-00000002\" in seg_path.name:\n        fired[\"done\"] = True\n        self._layout.trash_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        import os\n        os.replace(seg_path, self._layout.trash_dir / (seg_path.name + \".2\"))   # concurrent compactor move\n    return orig(self, seg_path, k)\n\nstore_mod.SpineStore._read_segment_tail = racy\ntry:\n    r2 = SpineStore(p)\n    got = [rec.seq for rec in r2.tail(40)]\nfinally:\n    store_mod.SpineStore._read_segment_tail = orig\n\ncontig = got == list(range(got[0], got[0] + len(got))) if got else False\nprint(\"raced   tail(40):\", got[0], \"..\", got[-1], \"len\", len(got),\n      \"contiguous\" if contig else \"NON-CONTIGUOUS\")\nprint(\"expected seqs   :\", list(range(35, 75)))\nprint(\"got seqs        :\", got)\nif got != list(range(35, 75)):\n    missing = sorted(set(range(35, 75)) - set(got))\n    print(f\"\\nFINDING CONFIRMED — tail(40) is WRONG. {"parentUuid":"98078a87-71d0-4c10-9c2e-52b79da32d54","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a66bbf52f5478e554","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdBw47iuciERt4YTmPKEp","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_012rVQHqYfwEqL8v8fyjeRZ3","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/tmp/claude-1000/-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main/7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27/scratchpad/s4_two_compactors.py","content":"\"\"\"Repro: two concurrent compactors race on the SHARED, deterministic temp path\n`<seg>.jsonl.gz.tmp`. P1 (the gzip build) is LOCK-FREE, so both processes/threads\ngzip.open(tmp, \"wb\") the same inode (each O_TRUNC) and interleave writes, then each\nos.replace(tmp, gz_abs). Outcomes: an uncaught FileNotFoundError from os.replace when the\npeer already consumed tmp, and/or a corrupted committed .gz. `os.replace(tmp, gz_abs)` has\nno try/except.\"\"\"\nimport tempfile, threading, gzip\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore\n\nfails = []\ncorrupt = []\nfor trial in range(12):\n    d = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=\"twocomp-\"))\n    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n    s = SpineStore(p, seg_max_bytes=0, seg_max_records=400)\n    s.migrate()\n    # one big sealed segment (400 fat records) so the gzip build takes long enough to overlap\n    for i in range(420):\n        s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"a\",\n                 payload={\"n\": i, \"pad\": \"lorem ipsum dolor sit amet consectetur \" * 8})\n\n    barrier = threading.Barrier(2)\n    errs = []\n    def run():\n        st = SpineStore(p)\n        try:\n            barrier.wait()\n            st.compact()\n        except Exception as e:                     # noqa: BLE001\n            errs.append(repr(e))\n    t1 = threading.Thread(target=run); t2 = threading.Thread(target=run)\n    t1.start(); t2.start(); t1.join(); t2.join()\n    if errs:\n        fails.append((trial, errs))\n\n    # is the committed .gz for seg-0 a VALID gzip that decompresses to the original records?\n    lay = SpineStore(p)\n    info = lay.segment_info()\n    seg0 = next((x for x in info if x[\"id\"] == 0), None)\n    if seg0 and seg0[\"codec\"] == \"gzip\":\n        gz = lay._layout.spine_dir / seg0[\"file\"]\n        try:\n            with gzip.open(gz, \"rb\") as f:\n                data = f.read()\n            nlines = len([x for x in data.split(b\"\\n\") if x.strip()])\n            if nlines != 400:\n                corrupt.append((trial, \"gz line count\", nlines))\n        except Exception as e:                     # noqa: BLE001\n            corrupt.append((trial, \"gz unreadable\", repr(e)))\n    # does a full read still work?\n    try:\n        seqs = [r.seq for r in SpineStore(p).iter_records()]\n        if seqs != list(range(420)):\n            corrupt.append((trial, \"iter mismatch\", len(seqs)))\n        ok, reason = SpineStore(p).verify()\n        if not ok:\n            corrupt.append((trial, \"verify fail\", reason))\n    except Exception as e:                          # noqa: BLE001\n        corrupt.append((trial, \"read raised\", repr(e)))\n\nprint(\"uncaught exceptions from concurrent compact():\")\nfor f in fails:\n    print(\"  trial\", f[0], f[1])\nprint(\"committed-data corruption / read failures:\")\nfor c in corrupt:\n    print(\"  \", c)\nif not fails and not corrupt:\n    print(\"  (none observed this run)\")\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":582,"cache_read_input_tokens":118198,"output_tokens":2139,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":582},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":2139,"cache_read_input_tokens":118198,"cache_creation_input_tokens":582,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":582,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdBw45YSK3YvZqhG8vZth","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"7f49a25e-9aea-40c0-bfd2-7e9f078f9ddd","timestamp":"2026-07-19T19:01:44.513Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

Let me now test whether the `iter_records` retry loop can exhaust its 6 attempts and raise `SpineError` on a healthy spine under sustained compaction (a slow reader)."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2554,"cache_read_input_tokens":123446,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2554,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdBwBQmmn2nUVodfvJWaa","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"2b47d43b-6f0b-4dd7-959f-622e7745c8bc","timestamp":"2026-07-19T19:03:46.142Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"2b47d43b-6f0b-4dd7-959f-622e7745c8bc","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a66bbf52f5478e554","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdBwBSTye2MuWeVXkdngk","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01DpzwskWuV7BB3GUN81mAY1","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/tmp/claude-1000/-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main/7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27/scratchpad/s4_retry_exhaust.py","content":"\"\"\"Probe: can a lock-free reader's iter_records exhaust the 6-attempt retry cap and raise SpineError on\na HEALTHY spine under sustained compaction? A slow consumer crossing many freshly-moved segment\nboundaries while a compactor churns. If it raises, a nonce-highwater/kill-switch state scan would\nspuriously fail-closed on a healthy spine.\"\"\"\nimport tempfile, threading, time\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore, SpineError\n\nd = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=\"exhaust-\"))\np = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\ns = SpineStore(p, seg_max_bytes=0, seg_max_records=5)   # tiny segments => many segment boundaries\ns.migrate()\nfor i in range(50):\n    s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"a\", payload={\"n\": i})\n\nraised = []\nstop = threading.Event()\n\ndef slow_reader():\n    r = SpineStore(p)\n    while not stop.is_set():\n        try:\n            out = []\n            for rec in r.iter_records():\n                out.append(rec.seq)\n                time.sleep(0.0008)                 # slow consumer: spans many compaction cycles\n            if out != list(range(len(out))):\n                raised.append((\"noncontig\", out[:3], out[-3:]))\n                return\n        except SpineError as e:\n            raised.append((\"SpineError\", str(e)))\n            return\n        except Exception as e:                     # noqa: BLE001\n            raised.append((\"other\", repr(e)))\n            return\n\nthreads = [threading.Thread(target=slow_reader) for _ in range(3)]\nfor t in threads:\n    t.start()\n\nt_end = time.time() + 25\nk = 0\nwhile time.time() < t_end and not raised:\n    for i in range(5):\n        s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"a\", payload={\"n\": 50 + k * 5 + i})\n    s.compact()\n    k += 1\n    time.sleep(0.001)\n\nstop.set()\nfor t in threads:\n    t.join(timeout=10)\n\nprint(\"compaction cycles:\", k)\nif raised:\n    print(\"OBSERVED:\", raised[:5])\nelse:\n    print(\"no exhaustion / no error observed\")\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2554,"cache_read_input_tokens":123446,"output_tokens":4137,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2554},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":4137,"cache_read_input_tokens":123446,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2554,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2554,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdBwBQmmn2nUVodfvJWaa","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"a7786e13-901a-4243-805f-106b25fd570b","timestamp":"2026-07-19T19:03:56.793Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"39d9cfd0-9765-4177-8f80-3d1465928af5","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a66bbf52f5478e554","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdBwJr3VtQ6z4cDBkJUfx","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_012pVkhyK4BT6m6Z2zDAaBPV","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/tmp/claude-1000/-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main/7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27/scratchpad/s4_reclaim.py","content":"\"\"\"Does compact() actually reclaim disk immediately? The test measures only segments_dir/*.gz vs\n*.jsonl and asserts after<before. But the superseded plaintext is MOVED to trash/ (not deleted) and\nonly reaped after SPINE_TRASH_GRACE_SEC (1h). So real on-disk bytes right after compact = gz + trashed\nplaintext, which is LARGER than before compact.\"\"\"\nimport tempfile, os\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore\nfrom sigil.spine.manifest import SpineLayout\n\ndef du(root: Path) -> int:\n    total = 0\n    for dp, _, files in os.walk(root):\n        for f in files:\n            try:\n                total += (Path(dp) / f).stat().st_size\n            except OSError:\n                pass\n    return total\n\nd = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=\"reclaim-\"))\np = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\nlay = SpineLayout.for_path(p)\ns = SpineStore(p, seg_max_bytes=0, seg_max_records=40)\ns.migrate()\nfor i in range(180):\n    s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"a\", payload={\"n\": i, \"text\": \"lorem ipsum dolor sit amet \" * 3})\n\nbefore_total = du(lay.spine_dir)\ngz_before = sum(f.stat().st_size for f in lay.segments_dir.glob(\"*.jsonl\"))   # what the test measures as \"before\"\ns.compact()\ngz_after = sum(f.stat().st_size for f in lay.segments_dir.glob(\"*.gz\"))       # what the test measures as \"after\"\nafter_total = du(lay.spine_dir)\ntrash = du(lay.trash_dir) if lay.trash_dir.exists() else 0\n\nprint(f\"TEST's measure:  segments *.jsonl before = {gz_before:>8}   segments *.gz after = {gz_after:>8}  -> 'reclaimed'\")\nprint(f\"REAL on-disk:    total before          = {before_total:>8}   total after (all dirs) = {after_total:>8}\")\nprint(f\"                 of which trash (superseded plaintext, lingers up to 1h) = {trash}\")\nprint()\nif after_total >= before_total:\n    print(f\"FINDING — after compact(), REAL disk ({after_total}) is NOT less than before ({before_total}); \"\n          f\"the plaintext lingers in trash for up to SPINE_TRASH_GRACE_SEC(=3600s). \"\n          f\"The test's segments-dir-only glob hides the trashed copy. So the grace runs from the\nsegment's ORIGINAL last-write time, not from when it entered trash. A sealed segment older than the\ngrace (the NORMAL case — you compact old segments) is reaped in the SAME compact() call that trashed it:\nZERO grace, not 1h. (Design says: 'the reaper deletes trash older than the read-grace, which must exceed\nthe longest lock-free read'.)\"\"\"\nimport tempfile, os, time\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore\nfrom sigil.spine.manifest import SpineLayout\n\nd = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=\"gracemtime-\"))\np = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\nlay = SpineLayout.for_path(p)\ns = SpineStore(p, seg_max_bytes=0, seg_max_records=5)\ns.migrate()\nfor i in range(12):\n    s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"a\", payload={\"n\": i})\n\n# make the sealed plaintext segments look \"old\" (sealed >2h ago) — the normal steady state\nold = time.time() - 2 * 3600\nfor f in lay.segments_dir.glob(\"seg-*.jsonl\"):\n    os.utime(f, (old, old))\n\ns.compact()   # default grace = 3600s (1h). Design intends 1h of trash grace after this move.\n\ntrash_files = list(lay.trash_dir.iterdir()) if lay.trash_dir.exists() else []\nprint(f\"trash files remaining right after compact(): {len(trash_files)}\")\nprint(f\"trash contents: {[f.name for f in trash_files]}\")\nif not trash_files:\n    print(\"\\nFINDING — the superseded plaintext was REAPED in the SAME compact() call, with ZERO grace, \"\n          \"despite the 1h SPINE_TRASH_GRACE_SEC. The reap clock is the segment's mtime (original write \"\n          \"time), not the trash-entry time; os.replace preserves mtime. The stated D2 read-grace \"\n          \"('must exceed the longest lock-free read') is NOT provided for any segment older than the grace.\")\nelse:\n    print(\"trash retained — grace held for these (mtime recent)\")\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":415,"cache_read_input_tokens":133261,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":415,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdBwNXwgmU95s4wV2Uqoe","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"14a1f424-ef62-4f60-bd54-5a042ce79090","timestamp":"2026-07-19T19:06:37.365Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"b4597a6e-21c4-4115-a505-d7f952a4a7d1","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a66bbf52f5478e554","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdBwUdx1Fcd3ivfp72hDb","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_011t9mRGXQReJAxAYZ2HyRhS","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/tmp/claude-1000/-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main/7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27/scratchpad/s4_zerograce_readers.py","content":"\"\"\"Is the trash grace actually load-bearing? Force IMMEDIATE reap (grace=-1) so the superseded plaintext\nis unlinked the instant it's trashed, then run the D2 concurrent-reader stress. If iter_records still\nholds, the grace provides NO reader protection (readers are covered by POSIX open-fd survival + the\nmanifest-reread retry, not by trash retention).\"\"\"\nimport tempfile, threading, time\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport sigil.spine.store as store_mod\nfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore\n\nstore_mod.SPINE_TRASH_GRACE_SEC = -1                # every trashed plaintext reaped immediately\n\nd = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=\"zerograce-\"))\np = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\ns = SpineStore(p, seg_max_bytes=0, seg_max_records=15)\ns.migrate()\nfor i in range(60):\n    s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"a\", payload={\"n\": i})\n\nerrors = []\nstop = threading.Event()\n\ndef reader():\n    r = SpineStore(p)\n    while not stop.is_set():\n        try:\n            seqs = [rec.seq for rec in r.iter_records()]\n        except Exception as e:                      # noqa: BLE001\n            errors.append(f\"iter raised {e!r}\")\n            return\n        if seqs != list(range(len(seqs))):\n            errors.append(f\"noncontig {seqs[:3]}..{seqs[-3:]}\")\n            return\n\nthreads = [threading.Thread(target=reader) for _ in range(6)]\nfor t in threads:\n    t.start()\nfor k in range(30):\n    _end = 60 + k * 15\n    for i in range(15):\n        s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"a\", payload={\"n\": _end + i})\n    s.compact()\n    time.sleep(0.003)\nstop.set()\nfor t in threads:\n    t.join(timeout=15)\n\nprint(\"iter_records errors under IMMEDIATE reap (grace=-1):\", errors[:5] if errors else \"NONE\")\nprint(\"=> grace is\", \"LOAD-BEARING (readers broke)\" if errors else\n      \"NOT load-bearing (retry + open-fd cover it; {"parentUuid":"1c6c4c33-0d5e-4c2d-84b5-b04b223f5c32","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a66bbf52f5478e554","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdBwXVXproRrTa5ZhbrFk","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_018JA3b8m8nccTKydeGc6qzg","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cd /home/kali/sigil && cat > /tmp/claude-1000/-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main/7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27/scratchpad/s4_corrupt_commit.py <<'PY'\nimport tempfile, threading, gzip\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore\nfrom sigil.spine.manifest import SpineLayout\n\ncommitted_corrupt = 0\ncrashes = 0\ntrials = 40\nfor trial in range(trials):\n    d = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=\"cc-\"))\n    p = d / \"spine.jsonl\"\n    lay = SpineLayout.for_path(p)\n    s = SpineStore(p, seg_max_bytes=0, seg_max_records=800)\n    s.migrate()\n    for i in range(820):\n        s.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"t\", actor=\"a\", payload={\"n\": i, \"pad\": \"x\" * 200})\n    barrier = threading.Barrier(2)\n    def run():\n        st = SpineStore(p)\n        try:\n            barrier.wait(); st.compact()\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n    t1 = threading.Thread(target=run); t2 = threading.Thread(target=run)\n    t1.start(); t2.start(); t1.join(); t2.join()\n    # inspect the committed seg-0\n    info = SpineStore(p).segment_info()\n    seg0 = next((x for x in info if x[\"id\"] == 0), None)\n    if seg0 and seg0[\"codec\"] == \"gzip\":\n        gz = lay.spine_dir / seg0[\"file\"]\n        try:\n            with gzip.open(gz, \"rb\") as f:\n                n = len([x for x in f.read().split(b\"\\n\") if x.strip()])\n            if n != 800:\n                committed_corrupt += 1\n        except Exception:\n            committed_corrupt += 1\n    # does a fresh full read + verify still hold?\n    try:\n        seqs = [r.seq for r in SpineStore(p).iter_records()]\n        ok, _ = SpineStore(p).verify()\n        if seqs != list(range(820)) or not ok:\n            committed_corrupt += 1\n    except Exception:\n        crashes += 1\nprint(f\"trials={trials}  committed_corrupt_or_readfail={committed_corrupt}  read_crashes={crashes}\")\nPY\nSIGIL_HOME=$(mktemp -d) ~/.sigil/venv/bin/python /tmp/claude-1000/-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main/7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27/scratchpad/s4_corrupt_commit.py","description":"Attempt to reproduce committed corrupt gz from concurrent compactors"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":273,"cache_read_input_tokens":140852,"output_tokens":3602,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":273},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":3602,"cache_read_input_tokens":140852,"cache_creation_input_tokens":273,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":273,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdBwXRWyvQMTh1by69vM3","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"686ca5bd-d6b7-456f-8fb5-ac1464074f61","timestamp":"2026-07-19T19:08:18.952Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"f6e1af81-f881-4604-8bd4-89b13148b3bc","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"8a1bd755-fe55-49e4-aa04-1c9cb10eb027","agentId":"a66bbf52f5478e554","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Q1HHVcSZnPcbhZCQ8Z7WL9","type":"tool_result","content":"        if not ok:\n            raise ValueError(\"arm request signature invalid\")\n        now = time.time() if now is None else now       # record-time freshness bounds aged-out replay bloat\n        ts_raw = core.get(\"ts\")\n        if ts_raw is None:                               # missing ts → invalid (was: float(None) TypeError below)\n            raise ValueError(\"invalid arm timestamp\")\n        try:\n            ts = float(ts_raw)\n        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n            raise ValueError(\"invalid arm timestamp\")\n        if not math.isfinite(ts) or not (abs(now - ts) <= ARM_FRESHNESS):\n            raise ValueError(\"stale or non-finite arm request — refused (not recorded)\")\n        sig = request.get(\"sig\")\n        # Bounded-window dedup OUTSIDE the append lock: collapses a rapid within-freshness replay flood\n        # (a flood is always recent). Aged-out replays are already refused above, so this need not scan\n        # the whole spine to bound bloat.\n        for r in self.store.tail(_DEDUP_WINDOW):\n            p = r.payload\n            if p.get(\"signal\") == ARM_REQUEST and p.get(\"pubkey\") == pub and p.get(\"sig\") == sig:\n                return r.seq                            # already recorded — idempotent\n        # record ONLY the signed core + pubkey + sig (never `**request` — no unsigned extras persisted)\n        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"DEVICE\",\n                                 payload={\"signal\": ARM_REQUEST, \"device_id\": core[\"device_id\"],\n                                          \"nonce\": core[\"nonce\"], \"ts\": core[\"ts\"],\n                                          \"ttl_seconds\": core[\"ttl_seconds\"], \"pubkey\": pub, \"sig\": sig,\n                                          \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"})\n\n    def panic_engage(self, *, by: str = \"phone\") -> int:\n        \"\"\"Halt the mesh from the phone. ANY engage halts (fail-safe) — no signature needed for the\n        SAFE direction. Release stays owner-only at the desktop (the dangerous direction is signed).\"\"\"\n        from ..governor.killswitch import SIGNAL as KS_SIGNAL\n        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"mesh\", actor=\"DEVICE\",\n                                 payload={\"signal\": KS_SIGNAL, \"state\": \"engaged\", \"by\": by,\n                                          \"reason\": \"panic halt from phone\", \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"})\n\n    def relay(self, text: str) -> str:\n=== _DEDUP_WINDOW ===\n20:_DEDUP_WINDOW = 2048                               # bound the replay-dedup scan to the most recent N records\n136:        for r in self.store.tail(_DEDUP_WINDOW):","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"e865039c-2162-4dd1-b678-56bdc7e82329","timestamp":"2026-07-19T19:09:54.709Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f6e1af81-f881-4604-8bd4-89b13148b3bc","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"c62626cd-6d4f-41cb-863b-af2639cdffae","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a66bbf52f5478e554","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdBwiPQYJHMaNm6uPccjS","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Confirmed the real-world impact of finding #1: `tail()` feeds the replay-dedup gate for **device-signed remote arm** (`bridge/daemon.py:136`, `arm_by_device`), a trust-widening action. A silently-holed window means a duplicate arm request can slip past dedup.\n\nAll repros are deterministic and the suite stays green. Here is my adversarial review.\n\n---\n\n# RED-PEN verdict: BLOCK — Slice 4 (gzip-on-seal + reader-grace), commit `4f3c58b`\n\nFour real defects. The compaction crash-safety of a **single** compactor holds and `iter_records`'s D2 grace holds — but `tail()` was left out of the grace, concurrent compactors crash, and the entire trash/grace/reaper subsystem is measured from the wrong clock **and** is dead machinery whose advertised safety it does not provide.\n\n## BLOCK-1 [HIGH] — `tail()` returns a non-contiguous / wrong window under concurrent compaction → replay-dedup bypass on the device-arm path\n`sigil/spine/store.py:818-843` (`_read_segment_tail`), `:803-816` (`tail`)\n\n`iter_records` got a 6-attempt manifest-reread retry for the D2 move race (lines 745-774). `tail()` did **not**. `_read_segment_tail` line 821 returns `[]` on `not p.exists()` with no retry. `tail()` resolves segment paths via `_segments_in_order()`, then a concurrent `compact()` moves a sealed plaintext to trash (`_trash_orphan_plaintext`, `os.replace` line 637) in the TOCTOU gap. The vanished segment silently yields `[]`, and `tail()` walks on to the next-**older** segment — producing a window with a hole plus stale older records.\n\nDeterministic repro (`s4_tail_det.py`, injecting the move at the exact resolve→read gap):\n```\nbaseline tail(40): 35 .. 74  contiguous\nraced    tail(40): 20 .. 74  NON-CONTIGUOUS\ngot seqs:  [20..29, 45..74]   # seg-2 (seqs 30-44) silently dropped; stale 20-29 pulled in\n```\n\nConsequence — not cosmetic. `tail()` is the substrate for the bridge daemon's replay-dedup on **device-signed remote arm** (`sigil/bridge/daemon.py:136`, `arm_by_device` — a TRUST-WIDENING action): `for r in self.store.tail(_DEDUP_WINDOW=2048)`. If the segment holding a prior `ARM_REQUEST` is dropped from the window during a concurrent `sigil spine compact`, the dedup returns \"not a duplicate\" and the same signed arm request is **recorded a second time** — a replay-dedup bypass on the arm path, within the freshness window. Any segment sealed longer ago than the grace — the *normal* case, since you compact old sealed segments — is reaped in the **same `compact()` call** that trashed it: ZERO grace.
